Result: 所有以基线预测初始化的首轮模型均优于基线和对照模型：XCAD池训练的模型宏平均Dice提升2.7点（95% CI 2.3–3.2），对照组下降0.6点；第二轮自训练无进一步提升。用50张全标注图像训练的外部模型生成初始标签时，XCAD Dice由0.623升至0.745（无需任何XCAD标签），但XACV未改善，因遗漏大主血管。

Conclusion: 自训练配合伪标签可有效提升外部数据的跨中心分割性能，但其收益在很大程度上取决于生成初始伪标签模型的敏感度；低敏感度导致的漏检会限制改进幅度。

Abstract: Deep learning models for coronary vessel segmentation in X-ray angiography often perform poorly on data from centres that were not represented in training. Apart from differences in equipment, the labels of the largest public dataset (ARCADE) are incomplete, as only the segments defined in the SYNTAX score are annotated. In this study, we evaluated whether self-training with pseudo-labels can improve performance on external data without additional manual annotation. Pseudo-labels were generated from two sources, namely the predictions of an nnU-Net model trained on ARCADE and a contrast map obtained by subtracting a pre-contrast frame from the same sequence. The nnU-Net predictions were extended along the contrast map, and pixels detected only in the subtraction image were assigned an ignore label and excluded from the loss. Six models were trained and tested on ARCADE and on two external datasets (XCAD and XACV). All first-round models initialised with the baseline predictions performed better than both the baseline and a control model fine-tuned for the same number of epochs. The model trained on the XCAD pool improved the macro-averaged Dice score by 2.7 points (95% CI 2.3-3.2), while the control decreased it by 0.6 points. A second round of self-training did not lead to further improvement. When an external model trained on 50 fully annotated images was used to generate the initial labels, the XCAD Dice score increased from 0.623 to 0.745 without any XCAD labels, although performance on XACV did not improve because large main vessels were missed. These results suggest that the benefit of self-training depends largely on the sensitivity of the model used to generate the initial pseudo-labels.

</details>


### [173] [Textual Response-Aware Guidance for Zero-Shot Adversarial Robustness of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40309)
*Yubo Cui,Xianchao Guan,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出文本响应感知引导（TRAG），利用预训练文本编码器对输入嵌入局部变化的响应约束视觉表示漂移，在对抗微调中兼顾鲁棒性与零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗微调方法依赖预训练模型引导以保持图文对齐，但其两类引导方式各有局限：视觉特征匹配覆盖全面却缺乏语义侧重；预测分布匹配有语义针对性但受候选文本与预测概率限制，均难以充分保持预训练图文关系，导致零样本性能下降。

Method: 分析视觉表示漂移并揭示两类引导的互补缺陷；提出TRAG：以预训练文本编码器对输入嵌入局部变化的响应为依据，围绕语义锚点构造锚点级约束（方向与幅度），再用预训练图文相关性聚合，得到图像自适应约束。

Result: 在多种架构与任务上的实验表明，方法在干净性能与鲁棒性能上均达到当前最优；进一步分析显示TRAG有助于保持预训练图文关系。

Conclusion: TRAG通过文本响应感知的图像自适应约束，有效缓解对抗微调中的表示漂移，实现鲁棒性与零样本泛化性能的兼顾。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit strong zero-shot generalization but remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Existing adversarial fine-tuning methods often use pre-trained model guidance to preserve image--text alignment while improving robustness. Our analysis of visual representation drift reveals complementary limitations of two representative forms of guidance. Matching visual features provides broad coverage by constraining all directions equally, but lacks semantic emphasis. Matching prediction distributions prioritizes semantically relevant directions, but remains limited by the candidate texts and prediction probabilities. These limitations can hinder preservation of pre-trained image--text relationships and degrade zero-shot performance. To address these issues, we propose Textual Response-Aware Guidance (TRAG), which constrains visual representation drift using the pre-trained text encoder's responses to local variations in input embeddings. Specifically, TRAG constructs anchor-level constraints from the response directions and magnitudes around semantic anchors, then aggregates them using pre-trained image--anchor relevance, yielding an image-adaptive constraint. Extensive experiments across architectures and tasks demonstrate state-of-the-art clean and robust performance. Further analysis shows that TRAG helps preserve pre-trained image--text relationships.

</details>


### [174] [Radiology Guideline-derived Decision Graph for Efficient Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40327)
*Xuewen Zhou,Zhen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GDGraph将临床指南编译为可执行的决策图，把指南编译与病例评估解耦，用System One快模型回答局部问题、由确定性遍历解析布尔逻辑，在347例三类影像指南上把准确率从57.3%提升到90.8%，优于检索增强GPT-4o（80.1%）与图引导+CoT的GPT-4o（82.7%），且延迟与成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像中的放射学风险分层与管理规划是关键下游任务，但自动化困难：指南判据分散在叙述文本、表格与交叉引用定义中，导致单次前向推理不可解释、难以直接落地。

Method: 提出Guideline-derived Decision Graph（GDGraph）框架，将指南编译与病例评估解耦。编译阶段：LLM按章节切块、抽取带定义链接的局部决策子图、聚合相关分支形成可执行决策图；推理阶段：快速System One模型回答局部化临床问题，由确定性遍历解析布尔逻辑，无需任务特定训练即得可追溯决策路径。

Result: 在涵盖肾囊肿、附件肿块、甲状腺结节指南的347例病例上，GDGraph将System One准确率由57.3%提升至90.8%，超过检索增强GPT-4o（80.1%）与图引导+链式思维的GPT-4o（82.7%），同时延迟与成本显著更低。

Conclusion: 将指南结构化为可执行决策图并结合轻量局部问答与确定性逻辑遍历，可在无任务特定训练下实现高准确、可追溯、低开销的影像指南自动化；代码将开源。

Abstract: Radiological risk stratification and management planning are essential downstream tasks in medical imaging. Automating these tasks is challenging because clinical guideline criteria are distributed across prose, tables, and cross-referenced definitions. We present Guideline-derived Decision Graph (GDGraph), a framework that decouples guideline compilation from case evaluation. During compilation, a large language model partitions guidelines into section-based chunks, extracts local decision subgraphs with linked definitions, and aggregates related branches into an executable decision graph. During inference, a fast System One model answers localized clinical questions while deterministic traversal resolves Boolean logic, turning opaque single-pass predictions into a traceable decision path without task-specific training. Across 347 cases spanning renal cyst, adnexal mass, and thyroid nodule guidelines, GDGraph improves System One accuracy from 57.3% to 90.8%, outperforming retrieval-augmented GPT-4o (80.1%) and graph-guided GPT-4o with chain-of-thought (82.7%) at substantially lower latency and cost. The source code will be available.

</details>


### [175] [RFW-A: Agentic Procedural Generation of 400K Articulated Assets over 500 Categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40333)
*Yuanbo Lyu,Changjian Pan,Saining Zhang,Yi Yang,Peishuo Li,Zhiheng Li,Jialu Liu,Junhao Chen,Jiawei Weng,Yupeng Gao,Weiqing Xiao,Wenyi Li,Jiarui Guo,Rui Zhang,Jingrun Du,Weiyi Lin,Zenan Meng,Hu Wei,Lin Qu,Zongzheng Zhang,Hao Zhao,Chaojian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RFW-A：将智能体程序综合与程序化生成结合，从已有铰接程序提炼类别级可复用生成器，低成本批量产出40万+可仿真铰接资产，并验证其在下游感知与真机策略迁移中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量铰接资产对具身感知、仿真与操作至关重要，但人工标注和程序化建模成本高；现有智能体式资产构建需为每个新资产重复推理与迭代，代价昂贵。核心观察是同一类别内资产尽管结构与外观各异，却共享构建逻辑，因此智能体合成与程序化生成具有互补性。

Method: 以现有铰接程序为起点，将可复用组件及其依赖组织为类别级生成器；智能体在采样配置与运动状态下对生成器进行测试与迭代精炼。生成器经构建验证后，通过程序化执行产生多样资产，无需额外模型调用或token开销。

Result: 生成器编写的API均摊成本为$10.62，在200个资产上摊薄至每个资产$0.05，而逐资产智能体合成基线为$1.24。构建了RFW-A-400K，涵盖500+类别、超40万可仿真铰接资产；实验显示结构/运动学有效性高，跨物理仿真器执行稳定；用其训练可提升已见与未见类别的部件级铰接估计；仿真中基于检索与程序编辑资产生成的策略可零样本迁移至真实铰接物体。

Conclusion: 智能体综合与程序化生成互补结合的范式能显著降低铰接资产规模化构建成本，同时保证有效性与可迁移性，并可直接赋能下游感知模型与真实机器人策略。

Abstract: High-quality articulated assets are essential for embodied perception, simulation, and manipulation, yet constructing them at scale remains difficult. Manual annotation and procedural modeling require substantial human effort. Recent agentic systems automate asset construction through program synthesis, but repeat costly reasoning and refinement for every new asset. Our key observation is that assets within a category can share construction logic despite variations in structure and appearance. Agentic synthesis and procedural generation are therefore complementary: agents can construct reusable programs, while procedural execution reuses the resulting logic across many samples. We therefore present RFW-A, which combines the two in a new articulated-asset generation paradigm. Starting from existing articulated programs, RFW-A organizes reusable components and their dependencies into category-level generators. Agents test and refine these generators across sampled configurations and motion states. Once constructed and validated, the generators produce diverse assets through procedural execution with no further model calls or token charges. The estimated API cost of generator authoring averages $10.62, amortizing to $0.05 per asset over 200 assets, versus $1.24 for the sampled per-asset agentic synthesis baseline. Using RFW-A, we built RFW-A-400K, containing over 400K sim-ready articulated assets across more than 500 categories. Experiments show high structural and kinematic validity and stable execution across physics simulators. Beyond asset validity, training with RFW-A-400K improves part-level articulation estimation on seen and unseen categories, while policies trained in simulation with retrieved and program-edited assets transfer zero-shot to real articulated objects.

</details>


### [176] [Wizard: Manipulate Any World in Real Time via Agentic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40339)
*Yuzhe Wu,Zijun Lin,Yeying Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Wizard：一个agentic游戏世界模型，以骨骼/几何结构代理为中间条件驱动视频生成，并配合自适应掩码与显式游戏状态维护，实现跨场景/角色/动作的零样本泛化与实时精确角色运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型与agentic框架虽将显式游戏逻辑与可控世界表示从视觉生成中解耦，但仅支持简单游戏设定与预定义运动模式，对未见环境与角色泛化差，实时交互性有限。作者希望实现玩家可自定义的、实时且机制一致的虚拟世界模拟。

Method: 构建覆盖750个场景、178个角色并含骨骼与几何信息的多样化数据集；将骨架与几何作为结构化中间代理来条件化视频生成模型；引入自适应掩码机制提升条件信号与生成视频rollout的对齐；设计agentic框架显式维护并更新游戏状态，使玩家可自由配置虚拟世界并对角色运动进行细粒度控制。

Result: 在保持视觉质量与游戏一致性的前提下，实现对角色运动的实时精确控制，并在新场景、新角色、新动作上取得零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将agentic控制与基于结构化代理的生成相结合，可实现玩家驱动、实时、可泛化且机制一致的世界模拟，拓展玩家自定义游戏创作的可能性。

Abstract: Recent advances in video world models have enabled the generation of increasingly realistic and interactive virtual environments, opening new possibilities for player-customizable gameplay. Agentic frameworks further advance this direction by decoupling explicit game logic and controllable world representations from visual generation. However, existing approaches support only simple game settings and predefined motion patterns, generalize poorly to unseen environments and characters, and offer limited real-time interactivity. In this paper, we introduce Wizard, an agentic game world model that enables real-time, precise control over character motions while generalizing zero-shot across novel scenes, characters, and actions. To achieve this, we construct a diverse dataset spanning 750 scenes and 178 characters, incorporating skeleton and geometry information as structured representations. We leverage these representations as intermediate proxies to condition a video generation model, together with an adaptive masking mechanism that improves alignment between the conditioning signals and generated video rollouts. Furthermore, we introduce an agentic framework that explicitly maintains and updates game states, allowing players to freely configure their virtual worlds and exercise fine-grained control over character motions while preserving visual quality and gameplay consistency. By combining agentic control with structured proxy-based generation, our framework unlocks new possibilities for player-driven game creation with real-time, generalizable, and mechanically consistent world simulation.

</details>


### [177] [HiFOR: Hierarchical Foresight with Action-Guided World Grounding for Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40373)
*Rongxing Ding,Hongyu Qu,Kehong Wu,Xudong Wang,Fan Zhang,Xinguang Xiang,Xiangbo Shu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 HiFOR 分层前瞻框架，通过分层前瞻预测与动作引导的世界检索，为机器人操作策略提供针对性的未来信息引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 方法将短期动力学与长期任务目标分开建模，且将预测未来直接注入动作策略，未充分考量其与当前动作需求的相关性。

Method: 1) Hierarchical Foresight Prediction：借助 Hierarchical Dependency Mask (HDM) 联合预测短期状态演化、语义里程碑与最终目标状态，建立跨时间尺度的依赖；2) Structure-Aware Latent World Modeling：沿时间、视角、空间维度组织未来隐表示以支持细粒度检索；3) Action-Guided Factorized World Retrieval：在去噪过程中依据演化中的动作表示自适应检索相关未来信息。

Result: 在两个仿真基准和六个真实机器人操作任务上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 统一建模跨时域未来预测并按需检索相关未来信息，可有效提升 VLA 模型的动作生成与任务执行表现。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated promising capabilities in robotic manipulation by integrating visual perception, language understanding, and action generation. Recent advances have further incorporated future prediction to enhance the understanding of environmental dynamics and task progression. However, existing methods often model short-term dynamics and long-term task objectives separately, while directly incorporating predicted futures into action policies without sufficiently considering their relevance to current action requirements. To address these limitations, we propose HiFOR, a hierarchical foresight framework with action-guided world retrieval. Specifically, Hierarchical Foresight Prediction jointly predicts short-term state evolution, semantic milestones, and final goal states through a Hierarchical Dependency Mask (HDM), establishing dependencies across temporal horizons. Building upon these predictions, Structure-Aware Latent World Modeling organizes future latent representations along the horizon, view, and spatial dimensions to facilitate fine-grained retrieval. Furthermore, Action-Guided Factorized World Retrieval adaptively retrieves relevant future information from the structured latent world based on evolving action representations during denoising, providing targeted guidance for action generation. Extensive experiments on two simulation benchmarks and six real-world robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [178] [Soil Moisture Estimation in Precision Agriculture: A Knowledge Distillation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40432)
*Mohammed Rakib,Vrinda Nagula,Arunkumar Bagavathi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出土壤湿度蒸馏框架SMD，用单模态教师模型通过知识蒸馏增强气象+视觉多模态土壤湿度估计，MAPE分别改进2%（土壤图像）和1.2%（作物图像）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准农业中的土壤湿度估计对灌溉、施肥与收获决策至关重要。传统方法依赖气象与土壤属性数据，近期方法引入航拍/地理空间影像等视觉模态，但高分辨率影像成本高、分析计算开销大，需在有限视觉输入下提升估计精度。

Method: 提出Soil Moisture Distillation（SMD）：一个基于知识蒸馏（KD）的多模态学习框架，引入单模态教师模型指导学生多模态模型，实现引导式单模态知识迁移；并对土壤区块图像与作物图像两类视觉模态进行对比分析。

Result: SMD优于传统多模态融合方法（如MIS-ME）及单模态模型；相比SOTA基线，土壤区块图像MAPE改进2%，作物图像改进1.2%；作物图像提供更优视觉线索，预测精度更高。

Conclusion: 通过知识蒸馏融合气象与视觉数据可有效提升土壤湿度预测，且单模态教师引导的多模态学习优于直接融合；作物图像是更经济有效的视觉线索选择。

Abstract: Accurate soil moisture estimation is critical for precision agriculture, influencing irrigation, fertilization, and harvesting strategies. Traditional methods primarily rely on meteorological and soil property data, while recent approaches incorporate visual modalities such as aerial and geospatial imagery. However, these high-resolution images are expensive and computationally demanding to analyze. In this work, we propose Soil Moisture Distillation (SMD), a Knowledge Distillation (KD)-based multimodal learning framework that leverages unimodal teachers to enhance multimodal soil moisture estimation. SMD surpasses not only traditional multimodal fusion techniques, including MIS-ME, but also outperforms unimodal models, demonstrating the benefits of guided unimodal knowledge transfer. Additionally, we conduct a comparative analysis of soil patches and crop images, showing that crop images provide superior visual cues, leading to improved prediction accuracy. SMD achieves a 2% improvement in MAPE for soil patches and 1.2% for crops over state-of-the-art baselines, highlighting the effectiveness of leveraging both meteorological and visual data through knowledge distillation for enhanced soil moisture forecasting.

</details>


### [179] [PhysInsert: Physically Plausible Object Insertion with Geometric Priors and Bidirectional Object-Scene Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40453)
*Yue Gong,Le Zhang,Rui Chen,Song Chang,Xiaochao Qu,Luoqi Liu,Ting Liu,Yebin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysInsert：基于Insert World数据集的参考物体插入框架，联合适应物体与场景，在跨环境/跨视角下提升图像保真、光照与姿态合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参考物体插入方法依赖掩码输入、生成式成对数据或视角变化有限的训练对，导致姿态与光照适应、以及物体-场景双向交互约束不足。

Method: 构建Insert World数据集（Blender渲染、严格对齐的反事实成对数据与物体参考，跨视角与光照）；提出Geometry-Guided Adaptation，将VGGT几何流与图像流、文本流在目标场景特征监督下耦合；辅以2D Object-Scene Spatial Supervision，联合监督物体占用与环境变化，并通过图文注意力将空间指令定位到物体区域。

Result: 在PhysInsert-Bench与DreamBooth上实验显示图像保真度提升；用户研究表明显式地更准确的放置、更好的光照融合、透视与姿态合理性。

Conclusion: 联合适应物体与场景、结合几何引导与2D空间监督，可有效实现物理上合理的参考物体插入，并具备光照与姿态适应能力。

Abstract: We present PhysInsert, a framework for physically plausible reference-based object insertion that jointly adapts objects and their surrounding scenes. Existing methods often rely on masked inputs, generation-based pairs, or training pairs with limited viewpoint variation, leaving pose and illumination adaptation and bidirectional object-scene interactions insufficiently constrained. We construct the Insert World dataset with strictly aligned Blender-rendered counterfactual pairs and object references across viewpoints and lighting conditions. Building on these data, Geometry-Guided Adaptation couples a VGGT geometry stream with image and text streams under target-scene feature supervision to guide spatially consistent insertion. Complementary 2D Object-Scene Spatial Supervision jointly supervises object occupancy and environmental changes while grounding spatial instructions in object regions through image-text attention. We introduce PhysInsert-Bench with dedicated cross-environment and cross-view cases requiring illumination and pose adaptation beyond appearance copying. Experiments on PhysInsert-Bench and DreamBooth demonstrate improved image fidelity; a user study further shows more accurate placement and improved lighting integration, perspective, and pose plausibility.

</details>


### [180] [A Unified Benchmark for Aerial-Enhanced Autonomous Driving: The KITScenes Aerial Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40455)
*Daniel Lengerer,Richard Schwarzkopf,Jonas Merkert,Fabian Immel,Rupert Polley,Frank Bieder,Jan-Hendrik Pauls,J. Marius Zöllner,Klaus Bogenberger,Christoph Stiller,Carsten Markgraf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KITScenes Aerial数据集，将航拍影像与车载多模态数据、位姿及Lanelet2地图标注空间关联，为地图构建、航拍辅助在线HD建图与跨视角定位提供统一基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航拍影像可为自动驾驶提供感知范围外的道路结构先验，但航拍建图、航拍辅助在线HD建图与跨视角定位通常在不同数据和基准上分别研究，缺乏统一的数据基础与评价框架。

Method: 在KITScenes Multimodal数据集基础上扩展：保留同步车载传感器数据、车辆位姿与Lanelet2地图标注，并引入空间关联的航拍影像与更高分辨率神经表征；在全部53个地图类别上，跨不同模型设计、空间范围与航拍分辨率进行建图评估，同时兼容三类别设置；并在地理不重叠条件下评估跨视角定位。

Result: 航拍上下文对细粒度与远距离建图尤为有利，在较大建图范围下使53类mAP提升一倍以上；更高分辨率航拍影像带来额外4.3 mAP（+24%）提升，收益延伸至交通灯等困难要素；地理不重叠条件下，改进的细粒度特征匹配基线达到5.13 m平均平移误差。

Conclusion: KITScenes Aerial为在互补的建图与定位任务中统一研究航拍与地面信息提供了公共基础与基准。

Abstract: Aerial imagery provides complementary spatial context for autonomous driving by exposing road structure beyond the immediate perception range of onboard sensors. Yet map construction from aerial imagery, aerial-assisted online HD mapping, and cross-view localization are typically studied on separate data and benchmarks. We introduce KITScenes Aerial, an extension of the KITScenes Multimodal dataset that augments synchronized onboard sensor data, vehicle poses, and rich Lanelet2 map annotations with spatially associated aerial imagery and higher-resolution neural representations, providing a common basis for a variety of research tasks. Our mapping evaluation focuses on all 53 KITScenes map classes across different model designs, spatial ranges, and aerial resolutions, while retaining compatibility with the widely used three-class setting. Representative baselines show that aerial context is particularly beneficial for fine-grained and extended-range mapping, more than doubling 53-class mAP at the larger mapping extent. Higher-resolution aerial imagery yields a further 4.3 mAP (+24 %) improvement, with gains extending to challenging map elements such as traffic lights. We additionally evaluate cross-view localization under geographically disjoint conditions, where an adapted fine-grained feature-matching baseline achieves a mean translation error of 5.13 m. KITScenes Aerial thus enables unified study of aerial and ground-level information across complementary mapping and localization tasks. Project page: https://kitscenes.com/aerial

</details>


### [181] [InfantNet: A Large Scale Dataset for Infant Body Pose and Shape Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40467)
*Zhipeng Hui,Jiahao Wang,Xun Dong,Guofeng Zhang,Xingrui Wang,Jiawei Peng,Qihao Liu,Xiaoding Yuan,Yi Zhang,Junjie Oscar Yin,Wufei Ma,Qinfan Lyu,Sara N. Lupolt,Keeve Edward Nachman,Adam Kortylewski,Alan Yuille*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InfantNet——迄今最大的真实图像婴儿数据集，含108,902张6-18月龄婴儿RGB图像（2D关键点全标注，11,642张含完整SMIL参数的3D姿态与形状标注），并建立婴儿2D关键点检测与3D姿态形状恢复基准。实验表明成人姿态估计器难以泛化至婴儿，而在InfantNet上微调可稳定提升性能，尤其3D任务中基于SMIL的估计达到与SMPL模型相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 婴儿姿态与形状估计对育儿、发育监测和医学诊断具有重要意义，但现有方法与数据集主要面向成人；由于婴儿在身体比例、关节活动范围及频繁自遮挡等方面与成人差异显著，直接迁移成人模型会失败，因此亟需大规模婴儿专用数据与基准。

Method: 构建InfantNet数据集：采集108,902张6-18月龄婴儿真实RGB图像，全部标注2D关键点，其中11,642张经多视角渲染与重投影误差过滤校验，附带完整SMIL参数的3D姿态与形状标注；采用迭代式标注流程保证2D与3D标签的高保真度；在此基础上建立2D关键点检测与3D姿态形状恢复的统一评测基准，并开展基线实验（成人SOTA模型直接测试与在InfantNet上微调对比）。

Result: 成人SOTA姿态估计器在婴儿数据上泛化能力差；在InfantNet上微调后2D与3D任务性能均一致提升，且3D姿态与形状估计提升更为显著，基于SMIL的估计精度可与SMPL类模型所报告结果相当。

Conclusion: InfantNet提供了大规模、高保真的婴儿姿态数据与综合基准，揭示成人模型迁移的局限并验证了婴儿专用数据微调的有效性，为婴儿姿态分析及相关健康应用研究提供关键资源。

Abstract: Infant pose and shape estimation is essential for applications in childcare, developmental monitoring, and medical diagnosis. However, existing methods and datasets are largely designed for adults, and direct transfer to infants fails due to substantial differences in body proportions, articulation limits, and frequent self-occlusion. To address this gap, we introduce InfantNet, the largest real-image infant dataset to date, comprising 108,902 RGB images of infants aged 6-18 months. Each image is annotated with 2D keypoints, and a curated subset of 11,642 images additionally includes 3D pose and shape annotations with full SMIL parameters, verified through multi-view rendering and reprojection-error filtering. We use an iterative annotation pipeline to ensure high fidelity across both 2D and 3D labels. InfantNet establishes a large-scale, comprehensive benchmark for infant 2D keypoint detection and 3D pose-and-shape recovery. Baseline experiments demonstrate that state-of-the-art adult pose estimators do not generalize well to infants, whereas fine-tuning on InfantNet yields a consistent improvement. The gains are even more pronounced for 3D pose and shape estimation, where SMIL-based estimation demonstrates robust performance with accuracy comparable to results reported for SMPL-based models. By releasing the InfantNet dataset and benchmark, we provide a vital resource for advancing infant pose analysis and related healthcare applications.

</details>


### [182] [Accurate MRI-free PET Quantification and Reliability Assessment via CT-to-MRI Synthesis and Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40478)
*Jungwoo Seo,Seung Yun Choi,Kun Ho Lee,Seun Jeon,Byoung Seok Ye,Jiook Cha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CT-to-MRI合成结合保形预测的无MRI淀粉样蛋白PET定量框架，在OASIS-3上SUVR与真实MRI高度一致，并提供可靠区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淀粉样蛋白PET的SUVR定量通常依赖受试者个体T1加权MRI进行解剖分割，但可用MRI并非总能获得，因此需要无需MRI的定量方案，并需对其结果可靠性进行评估。

Method: 采用切片一致的布朗桥扩散模型（Brownian Bridge Diffusion Model）进行CT到MRI合成，并通过多数据集训练、数据源条件化及扩展的多切片CT上下文增强解剖先验；合成MRI用于FreeSurfer解剖分区与PET定量；以深度集成不确定性结合保形预测（conformal prediction）校准，估计对应真实MRI SUVR的合理区间。

Result: 在OASIS-3的11C-PiB PET上，合成与分割保真度优于对照方法；平均皮层SUVR与真实MRI参考高度一致（MAE 0.016±0.015，Pearson r=0.9987），87例受试者在预设阈值下分类完全一致；保形预测经验覆盖率接近预设水平，90%目标覆盖率下87例中85例区间支持明确的淀粉样蛋白阳性判定；外部队列零样本评估显示模型可无需重训支持PET定量，但保形校准需目标队列重校准以恢复预期覆盖率。

Conclusion: 基于CT-to-MRI的无MRI PET定量可提供准确SUVR并显式评估其可靠性，是一种有前景的方案。

Abstract: Standardized uptake value ratio (SUVR) quantification of amyloid PET commonly relies on a subject-specific T1-weighted MRI, yet a usable MRI is not always available. We therefore combined CT-to-MRI synthesis with conformal prediction for MRI-free PET quantification with reliability assessment. A slice-consistent Brownian Bridge Diffusion Model was strengthened with multi-dataset training and data-source conditioning to enrich the anatomical prior learned from MRI, and with expanded multi-slice CT context to exploit the through-plane continuity of subject-specific anatomy in CT. Synthetic MRI was used for FreeSurfer-based anatomical parcellation and PET quantification, while deep-ensemble uncertainty was calibrated with conformal prediction to estimate a plausible range for the corresponding real MRI-based SUVR. On $^{11}$C-PiB PET from OASIS-3, the proposed method achieved the highest synthesis and parcellation fidelity among the compared methods. Mean cortical SUVR from the proposed synthetic MRI agreed closely with the real MRI-based reference (MAE $0.016 \pm 0.015$, Pearson $r = 0.9987$) and yielded the same amyloid-positivity classification as the real MRI in all 87 test subjects at the prespecified cut-off. Conformal prediction achieved empirical coverage close to the prespecified levels, and at 90% target coverage, 85 of 87 subjects had prediction intervals that supported an unambiguous amyloid-positivity decision. Zero-shot evaluation on an independent external cohort showed that the proposed CT-to-MRI model could support PET quantification without retraining, although conformal calibration required target-cohort recalibration to recover the intended coverage. These findings support CT-to-MRI-based MRI-free PET quantification as a promising approach for providing accurate SUVR measurements and an explicit assessment of their reliability.

</details>


### [183] [AVPS-360: A Spatial Audio-Visual Benchmark for Instance Segmentation and Tracking of Sound-Producing Persons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40480)
*Yifu Sun,Parthasaarathy Sudarsanam,Tuomas Virtanen,Archontis Politis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AVPS-360，首个结合一阶Ambisonics空间音频与360°视频的视听实例分割基准，基于STARSS23构建，含230段、10.5小时录制与31,678个实例掩码，并提供融合空间音频与视觉特征的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视听实例分割（AVIS）基准多采用单声道音频，缺乏声道间线索，难以在空间上区分同时发声的多个声源；且真实交互中声音间歇出现，长视频下的检测与身份关联面临挑战。

Method: 构建AVPS-360基准：以STARSS23为基础，将一阶Ambisonics空间音频与同步360°视频配对，共230段录制、总计10.5小时、平均时长164.0秒；标注31,678个人工复核的实例掩码，并关联声源身份与声音事件标签。同时提出基线模型，将空间音频特征与视觉特征融合，实现逐帧分割与跨帧身份关联。

Result: 实验表明，现有AVIS方法在长时、声音间歇出现的录制中于目标检测与身份关联两方面表现不佳；所提基线在两项指标上均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: AVPS-360弥合了AVIS与声音事件定位检测（SELD）之间的差距，为空间音频驱动的视听实例分割提供了新基准，并揭示空间线索对并发声源区分与长时身份跟踪的关键作用。

Abstract: Audio-visual instance segmentation (AVIS) segments and tracks individual sound-producing objects in videos. Existing benchmarks predominantly use monaural audio, which lacks inter-channel cues for spatially distinguishing concurrent sound sources. We introduce AVPS-360 (Audio-Visual Person Segmentation 360), a spatial audio-visual benchmark bridging AVIS and sound event localisation and detection (SELD). Built upon STARSS23, it pairs first-order Ambisonics audio with synchronised 360° video across 230 recordings totalling 10.5 hours. Recordings average 164.0 seconds. The dataset captures intermittent sound production in real-world interactions dominated by speech, alongside laughter, clapping, and footsteps. It provides 31,678 manually reviewed instance masks linked to source identities and sound-event labels. We also present a baseline that integrates spatial audio with visual features for frame-level segmentation and identity association across frames. Experiments on AVPS-360 show that existing AVIS methods struggle with detection and identity association in long recordings with intermittent sound production, while our baseline substantially improves performance in both aspects.

</details>


### [184] [MetaRefine: Score Refinement with Multi-Level Features for Image Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40487)
*Chuanzhi Xu,Chengkun Yue,Haolan Guo,Xuanhua Yin,Boyu Wei,Zihan Deng,Qiang Qu,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MetaRefine提出一种元数据感知的分数精修架构，利用原图几何信息与冻结模型的分数及多级视觉特征，通过轻量残差头预测分数修正量，在不改动原评分流程的前提下显著提升多项图像质量/美学评估指标，参数开销中位数仅1.11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像评估方法为适配不同输入尺寸需进行缩放、裁剪、填充或切块采样，这些操作会改变或掩盖原图的几何与构图线索；同时现有模型已编码丰富的多级视觉证据但未被充分利用。作者希望在不改变原有评分流程的情况下，将视觉证据与原图几何信息结合用于分数精修。

Method: 提出MetaRefine：一种元数据感知的分数精修架构，采用轻量残差头，以原图几何信息（原始尺寸、宽高比、构图）、多级视觉特征以及冻结模型的原始分数为输入，预测分数修正量，从而作为插件式后处理模块与现有评分管线解耦。

Result: 在21个数据集、100种配置上评估：88种配置的PLCC提升、85种配置的SRCC提升，平均相对增益分别为7.55%和7.10%；90种配置MAE降低、89种配置RMSE降低，平均相对降幅分别为23.27%和21.93%；参数开销中位数仅1.11%，并可在多个基准上进一步提升SOTA评估模型。

Conclusion: 原图几何与构图信息结合冻结模型的多级视觉证据可有效精修评估分数；MetaRefine以极低参数开销、不改动原流程的方式，一致地在排序相关与误差相关指标上带来提升，可作为通用插件增强现有及SOTA图像评估模型。

Abstract: An image's original dimensions, aspect ratio, and composition provide important cues that can affect its technical and aesthetic quality. Image assessment methods use resizing, cropping, padding, or patch sampling to accommodate different input sizes. However, these operations can alter or obscure original geometric and compositional cues. Existing assessment models also encode rich multi-level visual evidence that can be further extracted and exploited to improve existing models. This motivates us to combine visual evidence with original-image geometry for score refinement without changing the original scoring pipelines. In this paper, we introduce MetaRefine, a metadata-aware score refinement architecture whose lightweight residual heads predict score corrections from geometry, multi-level visual features, and the frozen model's original score. Across 100 configurations on 21 datasets, MetaRefine improves PLCC in 88 configurations and SRCC in 85, with average relative gains of 7.55% and 7.10%, respectively. It reduces MAE in 90 configurations and RMSE in 89, with average relative reductions of 23.27% and 21.93%, respectively. The median parameter overhead is only 1.11%. MetaRefine can further improve state-of-the-art (SOTA) assessment models on multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [185] [LucidLyra: Feed-Forward Generative 3D Scene Reconstruction and Joint Understanding via Multi-Task Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40495)
*Oliver Hahn,Rodrigo Marcuzzi,Sangeetha Grama Srinivasan,Alessandro Burzio,Sherwin Bahmani,Jiahui Huang,Stefan Roth,Shengyu Huang,Qunjie Zhou,Laura Leal-Taixé*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LucidLyra：从视频潜变量前馈式重建可直接查询语义与实例的3D场景


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式3D重建虽能从单张图像合成虚拟环境，但机器人等下游应用需要语义与物体身份信息；现有3D理解方法依赖多视角采集、泛化性差，或需昂贵的逐场景优化。

Method: 将相机可控视频扩散模型与冻结的2D视觉基础模型的互补知识蒸馏到统一模型，联合推断编码几何、外观、开放词汇语义与类无关实例线索的3D高斯；训练无需人工3D标注与多视角数据。

Result: 在合成的与真实图像条件化基准上，优于将SOTA 3DGS分割方法与生成式3D重建组合的基线，同时保持重建质量；潜变量形式可自然扩展到单目视频，重建跨时间的结构化动态3D场景。

Conclusion: 无需逐场景优化即可支持新视角渲染、开放词汇分割与物体分组，把可渲染但无结构的输出转化为可查询的3D世界，支撑3D场景编辑、3D资产挖掘与机器人仿真。

Abstract: We present LucidLyra, a framework that makes generatively reconstructed 3D scenes directly queryable for semantics and instances in a feed-forward manner from video latents. While generative 3D reconstruction can synthesize virtual environments from a single image, downstream applications in robotics and physical AI require not only visual realism but also access to scene semantics and object identities. Existing 3D understanding approaches, however, require captured multi-view scenes, have limited generalization capabilities, or rely on expensive per-scene processing and optimization. To bridge this gap, our method distills complementary knowledge from a camera-controlled video diffusion model and frozen 2D vision foundation models into a unified model to jointly infer 3D Gaussians encoding geometry, appearance, open-vocabulary semantics, and class-agnostic instance cues. The resulting scenes support novel-view rendering, open-vocabulary segmentation, and object grouping without per-scene optimization, while training requires neither manual 3D annotations nor captured multi-view data. Our method outperforms baselines combining state-of-the-art 3DGS segmentation approaches with generative 3D scene reconstruction on curated synthetic and real-image-conditioned benchmarks, while preserving reconstruction quality. Furthermore, our latent formulation naturally extends to monocular video, reconstructing structured dynamic 3D scenes across time. By turning renderable but unstructured outputs into queryable 3D worlds, LucidLyra enables downstream applications including 3D scene editing, 3D asset mining, and robotic simulation.

</details>


### [186] [CrossWeave: Cross-Modal Scene and Instance Retrieval from Sparse 2D-3D Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40525)
*Aadith Warrier,Gnana Prakash Punnavajhala,Siddharth Tourani,Muhammad Haris Khan,Avinash Sharma,Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CrossWeave框架，仅用稀疏2D-3D patch-point对应监督训练单一模型，统一完成场景级与实例级跨模态检索，无需整体场景/实例级对比目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态场景/实例检索方法仅在整场景或整物体层面学习对齐，缺乏对内部细粒度局部结构的监督；且场景级与实例级任务需分别训练模型，缺乏统一性与灵活性。

Method: 学习稀疏的patch-point对齐（2D图像块与3D点对应），不使用场景级或实例级对比损失；在此基础上引入基于最优传输（OT）的聚合器，对已对齐特征进行聚类，抑制结构冗余区域，为语义独特的局部特征释放描述子容量。

Result: 在ScanNet与3RScan上，仅用稀疏局部对应监督即可超越现有方法；加入OT聚合器后性能进一步提升；以单一模型同时取得场景级与实例级检索的SOTA，而现有方法需为两类任务分别建模。

Conclusion: 跨模态检索不必然依赖整体场景编码；稀疏局部对应监督是更有效且更灵活的替代方案，可支撑机器人及AR/VR中的空间推理。

Abstract: Cross-modal scene and instance retrieval aims to retrieve a corresponding 3D environment or object given query 2D images, serving as a foundational mechanism for spatial reasoning in robotics and AR/VR. Existing methods achieve this by learning alignment only at the level of whole scenes or objects, which provides no supervision for the fine-grained local structure within them. In this work, we show that supervision on a set of sparse 2D-3D correspondences, without scene- or instance-level contrastive objectives, is sufficient to train a single model for both scene- and instance-level retrieval. Specifically, we propose $\textbf{CrossWeave}$, a novel framework for cross-modal retrieval that learns sparse patch-point alignment, which alone outperforms existing methods. An optimal transport-based aggregator over the aligned features gives further gains by clustering structurally redundant regions, freeing descriptor capacity for semantically distinct local features. CrossWeave achieves state-of-the-art performance on both scene- and instance-level retrieval on ScanNet and 3RScan with a $\textit{single model}$, whereas existing methods require separate models for each task. Our results suggest that whole-scene encoding is unnecessary for cross-modal retrieval and that sparse local correspondence supervision is a more effective and flexible alternative.

</details>


### [187] [PhysCount: Do MLLMs Count Visual Appearances or Physical Entities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40530)
*Lidong Lu,Jiaxin Lv,Liangrui Dong,Xinyi Mao,Lidong Zou,Zhenquan Li,Tong Lu,Guo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhysCount基准，考察MLLM能否区分"图像中可见的表象"与"场景中真实存在的物理实体"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像显示的是外观，而计数常问的是物理物体。同一实体可能直接可见、经多次反射或透过玻璃可见，因此检测所有可见出现并不等于确定实体数量；现有视觉计数基准很少区分这两种操作。

Method: 构建PhysCount：546张真实图像、4,595道人工撰写问题，组织为651个目标特定组。每组标注相关表象位置、观察方式（直接/反射/玻璃等）及同一实体的表象归属；设计四类互补问题族——表象枚举、物理实体枚举、成像方式特异的计数、表象—实体对应关系。评估指标为各族准确率及整组全对的组比例，测试40个MLLM。

Result: 计数可见表象显著易于恢复其背后的实体身份；最强模型仅13.36%的组全对。当单一实体产生多个表象或表象跨越不同观察方式时性能下降；提高图像分辨率改善了视觉证据获取，但对整组全对准确率提升有限；联合回答相关问题则带来明显提升。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了"识别可见内容"与"正确恢复其背后物理实体"之间的能力鸿沟。

Abstract: Images reveal appearances, but counting often asks about physical objects. A single entity may be visible directly, in several reflections, or through glass, so detecting every visible occurrence does not determine how many entities are present. Existing visual counting benchmarks rarely separate these two operations. We introduce PhysCount, a benchmark for evaluating whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can distinguish what appears in an image from what physically exists in the scene. PhysCount contains 546 real-world images and 4,595 human-written questions organized into 651 target-specific groups. For each group, annotators locate the relevant appearances, label how each is observed, and identify appearances of the same entity. Four complementary question families test the enumeration of appearances, the enumeration of physical entities, image-formation-specific counting, and appearance--entity correspondence. We measure both accuracy on each family and the proportion of groups answered entirely correctly. Across 40 MLLMs, counting visible appearances is substantially easier than recovering the identities that connect them. Even the strongest model answers every question correctly in only 13.36\% of groups. Performance declines when one entity produces many appearances or when its appearances cross observation modes. Higher image resolution improves access to visual evidence but brings limited gains in complete-group accuracy, whereas answering related questions jointly produces a marked improvement. These results expose a gap between recognizing what is visible and correctly recovering the physical entities behind those observations.

</details>


### [188] [RADIOlogy: A Single-Pass Approach to Training-Free Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40580)
*Joaquín Figueira,Giacomo D'Amicantonio,Egor Bondarau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示视觉-语言模型中多头注意力池化的涌现性质，提出免训练、单次前向的零样本异常检测方法 RADIOlogy，通过对比评分实现检测，并结合 RADIO 特征进行密集异常细化，在医学等领域显著优于其他免训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本异常检测方法依赖复杂提示学习或计算昂贵的滑动窗口策略，以实现对细小异常的细粒度视觉对齐。其根源在于标准 CLIP 架构中 [CLS] token 注意力与空间 patch 池化难以捕捉细微的局部异常特征；而提示学习方法通常需要辅助数据集中的大量真值分割掩码，在专业领域往往不可得。

Method: 发现视觉-语言模型多头注意力池化具备开箱即用的零样本异常定位与检测能力，并将其转化为单次前向（single-pass）的检测技术 RADIOlogy：采用简单的对比评分，比较视觉特征与一组正常/异常提示之间的平均相似度，无需密集滑窗执行；同时利用 RADIO 模型的凝聚式（agglomerative）特征，通过密集异常细化流程增强像素级异常分割。

Result: RADIOlogy 无需密集滑窗执行，显著优于其他免训练基线；结合密集异常细化流程后，像素级异常分割得到增强；并证明免训练零样本视觉-语言异常检测技术族经最小改动即可成功应用于医学领域。

Conclusion: 多头注意力池化在视觉-语言模型中具有涌现的零样本异常定位与检测能力，可作为简洁高效的免训练单次检测基础；免训练零样本异常检测方法族具有跨领域（含医学）推广潜力。代码已开源。

Abstract: Current zero-shot anomaly detection methods often rely on complex prompt-learning techniques or computationally expensive sliding-window strategies to achieve fine-grained visual alignment to detect small-scale anomalies. This reliance stems from the fundamental limitation of standard CLIP architectures, where [CLS] token attention and spatial patch pooling struggle to capture subtle, localized anomaly features. While prompt-learning approaches aim to bridge this gap, they typically require a large number of ground-truth segmentation masks from auxiliary datasets, which are often unavailable in specialized domains. In this work, we show an emerging property of multi-head attention pooling in vision-language models: remarkable zero-shot anomaly localization and detection right out of the box. We translate this property into a single-pass anomaly detection technique, RADIOlogy, that does not require dense, windowed execution and significantly outperforms other training-free baselines. Instead, the method relies on a simple contrastive scoring approach that compares the average similarity between vision features and a suite of normal and abnormal prompts. Furthermore, we leverage the agglomerative features of RADIO models to enhance pixel-level anomaly segmentation using a dense anomaly refinement pipeline. Finally, we show how the vision-language family of training-free zero-shot anomaly detection techniques can be applied successfully to the medical domain with minimal modifications. Code is available at https://github.com/jacomof/RADIOlogy.

</details>


### [189] [Beyond Ground Truth: A Heuristic Unsupervised Quality Assurance Engine for Diagnosing Calibrational Blindness in Dental Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40587)
*Fatematuj Johura Mim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无真值分割质量检测引擎HUS-QA，用于牙科X线图像分割的临床质控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床中深度学习牙科X线分割可靠性不足：分割质量差时模型仍给出高置信度（校准盲视），且医生无真值掩膜可核对，标准置信度方法无法反映真实质量。

Method: 在PRAD根尖周牙科X线数据集上训练SimCLR自监督预训练的ResNet-18 U-Net生成分割掩膜；HUS-QA三阶段引擎仅用预测置信度、边缘锐度、形状连通性和面积尺寸惩罚四个简单指标，在无真值条件下评估掩膜质量。

Result: 标准置信度方法与真实Dice负相关（r = -0.1394，p = 1.7864e-3，ROC AUC = 0.4275）；HUS-QA与真实Dice正相关（r = 0.4647，p = 3.7510e-28），ROC AUC达0.8405。

Conclusion: HUS-QA可在无真值掩膜条件下有效评估分割质量，是临床质量控制中实用的工具。

Abstract: In real-world clinical settings, dental X-ray segmentation using deep learning methods suffers from reliability issues. Even when the segmentation quality is bad, deep learning models show high confidence in their predictions (calibrational blindness). Moreover, in real use, doctors do not have ground-truth masks to check their accuracy. To address this problem, we introduce a simple quality-checking engine called HUS-QA with three stages. We train it on the PRAD (Periapical Dental X-ray) dataset to build our SimCLR self-supervised ResNet-18 U-Net model, which generates the segmentation mask and then evaluates its quality. Our HUS-QA checks the segmentation mask quality without a ground-truth mask. Prediction confidence, edge sharpness, shape connectivity, and area-size penalty are the simple indicators considered in this research. Results show that standard confidence methods fail (r = -0.1394, p = 1.7864e-3, ROC AUC = 0.4275). By contrast, our HUS-QA shows a positive correlation with actual Dice scores (r = 0.4647, p = 3.7510e-28) and achieves a strong ROC-AUC of 0.8405. Therefore, HUS-QA serves as a practical tool for clinical quality control.

</details>


### [190] [HairDeck: Animation-Ready Stylized Hair Cards from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40599)
*Yuze He,Yixiang Wei,Wang Zhao,Chuhao Chen,Yanning Zhou,Yong-Jin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HairDeck 将风格化角色的发片发型建模为无序卡片集合，每张发片一个 token，用集合扩散 Transformer 从单图同时生成发片与驱动它的弹簧骨骼链，输出可动画资产；相比 CHARM，Chamfer 距离降 44%，方向误差减半以上，速度提升 33 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏与虚拟形象中风格化角色的头发由 artist-placed hair cards（发片）构成，并以 spring bones 驱动动画。现有图像方法只能生成写实头发或单一融合网格；唯一的发片生成器 CHARM 以自回归方式把发型解码为超长控制点序列，与发片本身是无序原始基元集合的本质不符，导致效率低、误差大。

Method: 提出 HairDeck：把每张 hair card 编码为单一 token，将发型视为无序集合而非长序列；采用 set diffusion transformer 对全部卡片联合去噪生成，并同时生成驱动发片的 spring-bone 链，得到可动并且可导出到下游格式的资产。

Result: 在 CHARM 自身测试集上，Chamfer 距离降低 44%，card-orientation 误差减少一半以上，运行速度快 33 倍；单图输入下优于 CHARM、image-to-3D 流水线与基于发丝（strand-based）的方法。

Conclusion: 将发片发型表述为无序卡片集合、以集合扩散一次性去噪，是风格化发片生成更自然且高效的建模范式，并可端到端产出可直接用于动画与下游管线的资产。

Abstract: The hair of stylized characters in games and avatars is built from hair cards: artist-placed ribbons whose geometry is the visible hair, animated by spring bones. Existing image-based methods produce realistic hair or a single fused mesh, while CHARM, the only generator of stylized hair cards, decodes a hairstyle autoregressively as a long sequence of control points. Observing that a card hairstyle is an unordered set of artist-defined primitives, we propose HairDeck, which generates it as one: each card is a single token, and a set diffusion transformer denoises all cards together. From a single image, HairDeck generates the cards and the spring-bone chains that drive them, yielding an animatable asset that exports to ready-to-use downstream formats. On CHARM's own test set, it lowers Chamfer distance by 44% and card-orientation error by more than half while running 33 times faster, and from single images it outperforms CHARM, image-to-3D pipelines and strand-based methods. Project page: https://hyzcluster.github.io/HairDeck

</details>


### [191] [Fight World Model: Customizable Combat through Visual Interaction Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40607)
*Jiahua Li,Zixu John,Tom Zhong,Fuping Wu,Tianhao Xu,Ruicheng Zhang,Jun Zhou,Zunnan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Fight World Model (FWM)，将规则驱动的战斗执行与视频渲染解耦：可执行引擎维护状态并解算交互，确定性编译器将其编码为时空对齐的交互代理，用于条件化视频渲染，从而在无需重训渲染器的情况下支持规则改动与新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可游玩、可编程的游戏世界需要持久状态、可执行规则与可扩展玩法；现有视频世界模型虽能生成看似合理的战斗画面，却难以保证受击反应与血量更新与游戏规则一致。

Method: 规则层：可执行引擎维护状态、解算交互结果；表示层：确定性编译器把结果与被尝试的动作编码为按角色身份与时间在空间、时间上对齐的交互代理（对齐动作/技能窗口、瞬时受击反应与持久血量），各线索可独立控制以渲染修改规则下的交互；身份绑定参考保持换边时的角色外观一致性；在 FightWorld Corpus（52 种技能效果、187 种外观变体、15 个竞技场）上训练。

Result: 无需重训渲染器即可支持伤害缩放、限定次数受击免疫、吸血、多阶段技能等原生游戏之外的机制，并支持角色与竞技场定制；在动作保真度、受击反应、血量状态一致性与角色外观保持上优于现有游戏世界模型。

Conclusion: 以“规则引擎+对齐交互代理”的解耦范式替代端到端视频生成来承载游戏逻辑，为可编程、可扩展的游戏世界模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: A playable, programmable game world must maintain persistent state, enforce executable rules, and support extensible gameplay mechanics. Existing video world models can generate plausible combat, yet struggle to keep hit reactions and health updates consistent with game rules. We introduce Fight World Model (FWM), which separates rule-governed combat execution from video rendering. An executable engine maintains state and resolves interactions. A deterministic compiler encodes these outcomes and attempted actions in a spatially and temporally aligned interaction proxy. The proxy aligns action and skill windows, brief hit reactions, and persistent health by fighter identity and timing. These cues can be controlled separately to render interactions under modified rules. Identity-bound references preserve character appearance across side switches. We train FWM on FightWorld Corpus, spanning 52 skill effects, 187 character-appearance variants, and 15 arenas. Without retraining the renderer, FWM supports damage scaling, limited-hit immunity, lifesteal, and multi-stage skill mechanisms beyond the native game. It also allows character and arena customization. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of FWM, which outperforms existing game world models in action fidelity, hit reactions, health-state consistency, and character appearance preservation. Our project is available at https://fight-world-model.github.io.

</details>


### [192] [L3G-World: Real-Time Infinite World Generation with Latent 3D Gaussian Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40614)
*Jinhyeok Choi,JoungBin Lee,Jongmin Lee,Minkyeong Jeon,Jiyoung Kim,Kaeun Han,Yoonjin Yoon,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出隐式3D高斯记忆L3G及其世界模型L3G-World，通过与视频DiT自回归交替更新紧凑场景记忆，实现实时、长时程空间一致的世界生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式世界模型依赖时序上下文或显式3D记忆，前者内存随生成流长度增长、后者需重编码RGB渲染结果，二者均难以兼顾长时程空间一致性与实时生成。

Method: 构建Latent 3D Gaussian Memory（L3G）：结合前馈重建效率与紧凑可快速渲染的场景表示，每个高斯存储VAE隐特征，可直接渲染进DiT潜空间而免去VAE重编码；在L3G-World中，记忆更新器与视频扩散Transformer自回归交替：在新观测区域生成高斯并精化已有高斯，使记忆随场景范围而非流长度增长。

Result: 生成质量与长时程空间一致性达SOTA；相较最高效的3D记忆基线，3D基元最多减少38.6×，记忆更新快3.2×，记忆渲染快359×，实现9 FPS实时世界生成。

Conclusion: 以隐特征化3D高斯作为可扩展场景记忆，配合自回归更新机制，可有效解耦记忆规模与生成流长度，在保证空间一致性的同时达成实时交互式世界生成。

Abstract: Interactive world models generate visual content conditioned on user actions, yet maintaining spatial consistency over long-horizon exploration in real time remains challenging. Existing approaches rely on either temporal context or explicit 3D memory, both of which scale poorly because memory grows with the length of the generated stream. Moreover, explicit 3D memories condition latent video generators by re-encoding RGB renderings, which precludes real-time generation. In this paper, we introduce a Latent 3D Gaussian Memory (L3G) that combines the efficiency of feed-forward reconstruction with a compact, rapidly renderable scene representation. Building on this memory, we propose L3G-World, in which a memory updater and a video diffusion transformer (DiT) alternate autoregressively to generate spatially consistent worlds. The memory updater spawns Gaussians in newly observed regions and refines existing ones, so that the memory grows with scene extent rather than stream length. Each Gaussian further stores a VAE latent feature, allowing the memory to be rendered directly into the DiT's latent space without VAE re-encoding. Experiments show that L3G-World achieves state-of-the-art generation quality and long-horizon spatial consistency, with up to $38.6\times$ fewer 3D primitives, $3.2\times$ faster memory updates, and $359\times$ faster memory rendering relative to the most efficient prior 3D-memory baseline for each metric, enabling real-time world generation at 9 FPS.

</details>


### [193] [Difficulty Calibration Across Time for Membership Inference in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40616)
*Michael Joseph,Yedid Hoshen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对扩散模型成员推理攻击（MIA）中"重建损失低即成员"的判据失效问题，提出沿训练时间与扩散时间两个维度进行难度校准：RMC通过与早期检查点对比隔离新记忆数据，TSC则利用单模型在不同噪声层级上的损失对比衰减图像固有难度。方法显著超越现有SOTA，并可跨x/v/ε参数化泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MIA以重建损失打分并将低损失图像判为成员，但图像本身的固有重建难度差异较大，导致简单的未见图像可能比复杂的训练图像损失更低，产生大量误判。核心动机是区分"记忆"与"图像固有难度"，即做难度校准。

Method: 提出沿两个时间轴的校准机制：(1) Reference-Model Calibration (RMC)：将微调模型的损失与早期检查点对比，隔离出新增记忆的数据，无需文本描述即可实现近乎完美的审计；(2) Temporal Self-Calibration (TSC)：仅用单一模型，跨扩散时间步校准。其关键观察是图像难度在各噪声水平上持续存在，而记忆则局部于特定时间步，因此将目标时间步的损失与相邻时间步损失比较，用模型自身轨迹衰减图像难度。

Result: 实证结果表明，所提方法大幅超越现有最先进方法；且不同于多数现有攻击，方法在$x$-、$v$-和$\varepsilon$-prediction等不同模型参数化下均能泛化。

Conclusion: 难度校准是提升扩散模型成员推理有效性的关键：沿训练时间（RMC）与扩散时间（TSC）两条轴进行校准，可有效分离记忆信号与固有难度，TSC更以单模型实现无需检查点的实用攻击；方法具备跨参数化的通用性。

Abstract: Modern diffusion models are trained on massive datasets that often include copyrighted or private images. Membership inference attacks (MIAs) address this by testing whether a given image was in a model's training set. The standard approach scores an image by its reconstruction loss and flags low-loss images as members. This often fails because some images are simply easier to reconstruct than others, so a simple unseen image can score a lower loss than a complex training image. Difficulty calibration separates memorization from how difficult an image is to begin with. We show how to calibrate along two axes of time: training time and diffusion time. First, across training time, we introduce Reference-Model Calibration (RMC), which compares a fine-tuned model's loss against an earlier checkpoint. This cleanly isolates newly memorized data and gives near-perfect auditing, even without text captions. Since earlier checkpoints are not always available, we next present Temporal Self-Calibration (TSC), which calibrates across diffusion time using only a single model. Our key observation is that an image's difficulty persists across noise levels, whereas memorization is localized in time. By comparing the loss at a target timestep against neighboring timesteps from the same model, TSC attenuates image difficulty using the model's own trajectory. Empirically, our methods beat the state of the art by a wide margin, and unlike most existing attacks, generalize across different model parameterizations such as $x$-, $v$-, and $ε$-prediction models. Code is available at https://github.com/MichaelJoseph777/TSC.

</details>


### [194] [Probing Spatial State Representations in Visual Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40636)
*Baiqiao Yin,Zhongang Cai,Ruisi Wang,Qiwei Liang,Lei Yang,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在十个视觉基础模型的冻结特征上，用四个层级（空间结构、物体持续性、预测性空间更新、可行动性）的探针测试其是否具备持久且可随自运动更新、并指导动作的空间状态表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察者只能看到局部世界，却需追踪视野之外的事物，因此空间感知需要一个在自运动下持续更新的空间状态，而非仅识别单幅图像。作者据此追问：视觉基础模型是否提供了支持这种状态的表征。

Method: 在静态室内环境的流式自我中心观测下，对十种表征做冻结特征探针，覆盖空间结构、物体持续性、预测性空间更新与可行动性四个层级；并设计匹配对照，将读出准确性与对视觉历史和运动的依赖分离开来。

Result: 能力画像随任务、网络层与前缀长度而变化：可见场景的强读出并不保证物体持续性或未来特征检索的准确性。原生未来预测保留了可恢复的空间结构，但在真实特征上训练的空间读出器不能原样迁移到预测特征。目标相关的候选选择未能建立可靠的连续动作效用：预测动作会随目标变化，但给出正确目标并不能稳定提升进展。物体持续性与可行动性依赖相机位姿读出，而特征预测依赖可见场景读出。

Conclusion: 评估空间表征不应只看"能从中学到什么"，还应看信息能否在观测之外被维持、被预测性更新、并被用于目标导向动作。

Abstract: An observer sees only part of the surrounding world, yet must keep track of what lies beyond the current view. Spatial perception therefore involves more than recognizing individual images: it calls for a persistent spatial state that is updated under self-motion and used to guide action. Do visual foundation models provide representations that support such a state? We study this question through four levels of spatial capability---spatial structure, object persistence, predictive spatial update, and actionability---using frozen-feature probes on ten representations under streaming egocentric observations of static indoor environments. The resulting capability profiles vary across tasks, network layers, and prefix lengths: strong readout of the visible scene does not ensure accurate object persistence or future-feature retrieval. Matched controls separate readout accuracy from reliance on visual history and motion. Native future prediction retains recoverable spatial structure, but a spatial reader trained on real features does not transfer to predicted features unchanged. Finally, goal-dependent candidate selection does not establish reliable continuous action utility: predicted actions respond to goal changes, yet supplying the correct goal does not reliably improve progress. Across the four levels, object persistence and actionability build on camera-pose readout, whereas feature prediction builds on visible-scene readout. Together, these findings motivate evaluating spatial representations not only by what can be decoded from them, but by whether that information is maintained beyond observation, updated predictively, and used for goal-directed action.

</details>


### [195] [SonoWorld4D: From One Video to a 4D Audio-Visual Scene via Diffusion Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40640)
*Derong Jin,Xiyi Chen,Ming C. Lin,Ruohan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Video-to-4DAVScene 新任务与 SonoWorld4D 框架，从单目视频生成可探索、时变的4D视听场景（含360°静态背景、动态发声对象轨迹与多源空间声场），支持实时新视角视觉与空间音频渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉场景生成已从静态3D扩展到由少量输入生成动态4D场景，但声音虽与动态对象和事件内在耦合，却在先前工作中基本未被建模；因此需要将视频转化为兼具视觉与物理合理空间音频的4D场景。

Method: 输入单目视频：生成360°静态背景；重建显式动态且发声前景对象的运动轨迹；通过"双空间音频扩散同步"合成跨多声源一致、物理合理的声场；最终场景支持实时新视角视觉与空间音频渲染。

Result: 在真实动态场景与用户研究中评估，SonoWorld4D 生成的4D视听场景具有感知真实、物理合理且与场景几何和视觉内容一致的空间音频。

Conclusion: 将动态发声对象与空间声场纳入4D场景生成是可行且有效的，可实现视听一致、可实时探索的4D视听场景，为后续视听联合场景建模提供新任务与基线框架。

Abstract: Recent progress in visual scene generation has extended beyond static 3D scenes toward the more challenging problem of generating dynamic 4D scenes from minimal input. However, sound, despite being intrinsically coupled with dynamic objects and events, remains largely unmodeled in prior work. We introduce Video-to-4DAVScene, a new task that turns a video into an explorable, time-varying 4D audio-visual scene, and present the SonoWorld4D framework for this task. Given a monocular video, our method generates a 360 static background, reconstructs explicit motion trajectories of dynamic, sound-emitting foreground objects, and synthesizes a physically-grounded sound field consistent across multiple sources through dual-space audio diffusion synchronization. The resulting 4D scene supports real-time novel-view visual and spatial audio rendering. Evaluation on real-world dynamic scenes and a user study demonstrates SonoWorld4D generates 4D audio-visual scenes with perceptually realistic, physically grounded spatial audio consistent with scene geometry and visual content.

</details>


### [196] [Luggage re-identification: A study of strategies towards a real-world application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40643)
*Quentin Dhôte,Romaric Audigier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究从多路视频流出发的端到端行李重识别（ReID）流程，并在真实场景数据集上评估不同变体，以提升实际应用中的ReID性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行李ReID方法多在相对干净的条件下基于裁剪图像的表观匹配，而真实场景存在频繁遮挡、背景杂乱与拥挤，性能显著下降；同时，利用来自不同视频流的多镜头、多视角互补性的策略被忽视。

Method: 构建从多视频流检测并重识别行李的端到端流水线，并比较多种变体；在真实环境采集的基准数据集上进行评估，重点考察同一行李不同视角与镜头的选择与融合策略，以及在不同环境数据集上训练以改善整体ReID性能。

Result: 在真实环境基准数据集上对多种流水线变体进行了对比评估，回答了视角/镜头选择与融合、以及跨环境数据集训练对整体ReID性能的影响（摘要未给出具体数值结果）。

Conclusion: 多镜头、多视角互补信息与合理的选择融合策略，以及跨环境数据集训练，是提升真实场景行李ReID实际性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Luggage re-identification (ReID) is a critical task in video surveillance for forensics, security or border management (suspicious or abandoned luggage recognition), as well as luggage management (lost luggage recovery). It enables the association of observations of the same luggage across disjoint camera views in complex environments such as airports, train stations and border crossing. While existing approaches primarily focus on appearance-based cropped image matching under relatively clean conditions, real-world scenarios are characterized by frequent occlusions, cluttered background and crowded scenes which significantly degrade performance. Yet, strategies leveraging the complementarity of multiple shots and views from different video streams is overlooked. In this paper, we study strategies for an end-to-end pipeline that operates from multiple video streams to detect and re-identify luggage. We evaluate different variants on a benchmark dataset acquired in real-world environments to address the questions of selecting and merging different views and shots of the same luggage as well as training on different environment datasets for an overall ReID performance improvement in practical applications.

</details>


### [197] [Read, Allocate, Route: Role-Coupled Tokenization for Pixel-Space Generative Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40647)
*Jiongfan Yang,Weichen Fan,Haiwen Diao,Zhongang Cai,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpecSlot 是一种像素空间 rectified-flow Transformer，通过将每个空间锚点扩展为多个 DCT 系数槽，把输出细节与全局注意力序列长度解耦，实现更高频细节建模而不增加全局序列开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有像素空间生成 Transformer 通常每个图像 patch 对应一个 token，因此捕捉更细细节需要更多 token，导致全局注意力代价急剧上升。作者观察到精细细节并不必然需要更多全局通信：低频结构依赖全局交换，高频细节可在局部建模。

Method: 提出 SpecSlot：每个空间锚点扩展为多个 slot，各 slot 分配互不重叠的 DCT 系数频带，将锚点密度与频谱细分解耦；仅每个锚点的最低频 slot 参与全局注意力，所有 slot 进行局部交互；引入 spatial head 对频谱预测施加局部校正；生成状态、目标与损失保持在像素空间；基于 rectified flow。

Result: ImageNet-256 上与 FLOPs 匹配的 JiT 对照取得相当最终 FID 且收敛更快；ImageNet-512 上 FID 提升 2.84、FLOPs 减少 3.9%，与 JiT-B/16 相差仅 0.69 FID 而成本低 38.2%；文生图任务在 DPG-Bench 上比 JiT-B/8 高 1.06，前向成本低 40.6%。

Conclusion: 用频带划分替代 token 增密可有效扩展细节生成，同时显著降低全局注意力与整体计算成本，为像素空间生成 Transformer 提供高效新范式。

Abstract: Pixel-space generative Transformers typically use one token per image patch, so capturing finer detail requires more tokens and makes global attention increasingly expensive. We observe that finer detail does not necessarily require more global communication: low-frequency structure benefits from global exchange, while high-frequency detail can be modeled locally. Based on this insight, we introduce SpecSlot, a pixel-space rectified-flow Transformer that decouples output detail from the length of the global attention sequence. SpecSlot expands each spatial anchor into multiple slots, each assigned a disjoint band of DCT coefficients; this separates anchor density from spectral subdivision. Only the lowest-frequency slot from each anchor participates in global attention, while all slots interact locally. This allows the model to increase spectral detail without enlarging the global sequence. A spatial head further adds a local correction to the spectral prediction, while the generative state, target, and loss remain in pixel space. On ImageNet-256, SpecSlot achieves a comparable final FID to a FLOPs-matched JiT control while converging faster. On ImageNet-512, it improves FID by 2.84 over the control with 3.9% fewer FLOPs, and comes within only 0.69 FID of JiT-B/16 at 38.2% lower cost. On text-to-image generation, SpecSlot surpasses JiT-B/8 on DPG-Bench by 1.06 at 40.6% lower forward cost.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Cassette-Editor: A Video Editing Agent That Edits the Way You Do](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37690)
*Zhixin Zeng,Jiahao Zhong*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出 Cassette-Editor：一个"一读一写"双工具的持久化工件（时间线）视频编辑智能体，所有写操作经统一执行器/规则/提交服务原子提交或被拒并返回定位诊断，模型保留创作决策与恢复权。受控实验中，真实模型对 277 处被破坏的自身提交进行修复，几乎全部成功；精确定位使单次修正所需输入 token 减少一半以上，且连带误改其他参数的情况由 7 例降至 1 例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体越来越多地在持久化工件（如代码仓库、视频时间线）上调用工具，每步结果决定下一步。视频编辑是苛刻案例：画面呈现依赖每个片段的裁剪、变速、裁切与嵌套，素材知识仅对特定用法成立，且非法编辑会使时间线结构性损坏。现有智能体依赖批评者智能体、审核者或受限输出格式来保证编辑合法，代价高且限制表达。

Method: 刻意简化接口：单一只读工具（关联素材存储分析与时间线上每次用法）+ 单一写入工具。所有写入与用户手动编辑共用同一执行器、规则与提交服务，要么原子提交，要么被拒绝并返回带定位的诊断与修复选项；模型负责创作选择与恢复策略。受控实验：让真实模型面对 277 处被破坏的自身已提交编辑，比较"完全反馈"与"最小反馈（定位）"两种条件。

Result: 模型在两种反馈下几乎修正了全部调用；提供问题定位后，单次修正所需的输入 token 降至不足原来的一半；对模型此前写入的其他数值造成的连带破坏由 7 例降至 1 例。

Conclusion: 将合法性约束下沉到持久化工件的写路径（统一执行器+原子提交+定位诊断），而非依赖外部审阅智能体或受限输出，可在保持模型创作自由的同时显著提升编辑可靠性与修正效率；工具接口的"少而稳"与诊断的"精确"是该模式的关键收益来源。

Abstract: Language agents increasingly work by calling tools on a persistent artifact, such as a code repository, and the result of each call shapes their next step. Video editing is a demanding case of this pattern: what a frame shows depends on how every clip is trimmed, retimed, cropped, and nested, so knowledge about the footage holds only for particular uses of it, and an invalid edit can leave the timeline structurally broken. Existing video editing agents keep their edits valid with critic agents, reviewers, or restricted output formats. We present Cassette-Editor, a video editing agent with a deliberately simple interface: one tool reads the project and one tool writes to it. Every write passes through the same executor, rules, and commit service as the user's manual edits, and either commits atomically or is rejected with located diagnostics and repair options, while the model keeps the creative choices and decides how to recover. Reads relate stored analysis of the footage to each use of it on the timeline. In a controlled experiment, a real model is shown 277 of its own committed edits after damaging them. It corrected nearly every call with full or minimal feedback, but locating each problem cut the input tokens a correction needed to less than half, and its corrections dropped other values the model had written in one case rather than seven.

</details>


### [199] [TSM diff: A new Parallel-Tree-Traversal Algorithm for the Comparison of Music Score Files](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38545)
*Xiaobo Wang,Florent Jacquemard*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出基于Tree Score Model（TSM）图结构抽象表示的乐谱比较算法与工具，可输出差异列表、时间距离、表面距离并归一化为相似度，已用C++实现并提供Python接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乐谱内容复杂且呈层次化结构，难以像文本那样用diff类工具比较，缺乏类似的乐谱比对工具。

Method: 将MusicXML、MEI等不同编码的乐谱转换为TSM图结构抽象表示，再对两份乐谱联合计算：按时间位置定位的差异列表、累计差异时段时长的时间距离、基于非时间相关记谱属性的编辑距离（surface distance），后两者归一化合并为相似度距离。C++实现并提供Python模块。

Result: 初步实验在挑战性数据上表现出很高效率；以莫扎特钢琴奏鸣曲不同版本、不同演奏者对同一爵士标准曲的独奏转录为概念验证演示。

Conclusion: 该工作为多种编码的数字化乐谱提供了可行且高效的比较工具，兼顾时间与记谱属性两个维度，并具备良好实用性。

Abstract: Music score files are more difficult to compare than their text-based equivalents, as their content is more complex and organised into hierarchical structures. This explains the lack of tools for processing music scores similar to the widely used old Unix diff command. We propose a new algorithm and tool for comparing digital music scores in various encodings, such as MusicXML or MEI, based on a graph-structured abstract representation called Tree Score Model (TSM). Given two scores, after conversion into TSM, our procedure calculates jointly: A list of differences, localised by time positions in the input scores; A time distance, cumulated duration of time-spans where scores differ; An edit distance, called surface distance, based on notational attributes not related to time. The two later measures are normalised and combined into a similarity distance between scores. Our procedure has been implemented in C++ and is available as a Python module. Our first experiments have shown a very good efficiency even on challenging data. As a proof of concept, we propose two demos based on different versions of some Mozart's piano sonata, and transcriptions of monophonic soli on the same jazz standards by different performers. https://tsm-diff-lbd-7a7e2a.gitlab.io/demo.html

</details>
